# RELEASE_LEDGER.md — one row per release, per deployable unit


## R-2026-09-22-B — `FIN-B2a + F2`: the salon can finally SEE the Stripe fee on a booking · 1-site release (`hosting:salown`) · **LIVE_VERIFIED**

- **Why:** `R-2026-09-22-A` started recording captures and actual fees the same morning, and by midday the ledger held a real one — but nothing in the product displayed it. B2a is the reader (`settlementFacts.ts`) and the booking-detail block (`OnlinePaymentFees.tsx`) that turn that record into something the owner can read. **Display only:** no P&L line, no Bank Balance figure, no fee day, no period, no write of any kind. `FIN_B2A_RELEASE_PREFLIGHT.md`, `PROCESSOR_FEES_PLAN.md` §1.3.
- **Owner approval (2026-09-22), four decisions taken explicitly:** ① **do not use `origin/main`** ② cut the candidate from the **live Admin source `5be583c`**, so the Reports passive-barber change and the Staff override-flow copy are not carried ③ **fix F2 inside this release** ④ **F3 and F5 out of scope**, recorded as follow-ups. Then a separate, explicit approval of the deploy itself, naming `hosting:salown` alone and recording the rollback identity in advance.
- **Source:** salown-app **`daf124499c836d7896fbb5ea794072767d7a2e17`**, branch `release/fin-b2a-only` — three commits on the LIVE Admin source `5be583c`: `a4ad274` (= `74922bd`), `f2cd77a` (= `9a4925c`) and `daf1244` (F2). The two replays produced **one** conflict, two adjacent import lines in `BookingDetailPanel.tsx`, resolved by keeping both; the five non-panel B2a files are byte-identical to `origin/main`'s. **`main` was not merged and did not move** — still `55ec9df` before and after. A branch push triggers no workflow: both are `branches: [main]` only.
- **Scope — six files, all under `src/`, +902/−4.** `settlementFacts.ts` (new reader), `OnlinePaymentFees.tsx` (new block), `BookingDetailPanel.tsx` (mount + read-once + one refund summary), and three test files. No writer, no `functions/`, no `firestore.rules`, no `firestore.indexes.json`, no flag, no date policy, no translation, no `firestoreActions.ts`, no `src/staff/**`, no `hosting/**`.
- **F2 — an abandoned checkout is not a payment.** The reader treated `paymentProvider: 'EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT'` as evidence a Stripe payment exists. That label is written when a booking is *sent* to checkout (`functions/src/bookings/createBooking.ts`), so a customer who abandoned the Stripe page left a booking drawing a full fee section with **no amount in it**. **The trap that changed the fix:** re-reading the production sample *with* `stripeSessionId` showed **3 of the 4** abandoned bookings carry one — it is written at session creation, so treating it as evidence would have left the defect almost entirely unfixed. Evidence is what the **webhook** writes: a ledger record, `stripeAmountPaid`, or `stripePaymentIntent`. That is the principle `functions/src/checkout/executor.ts` already states for this rail — *"`stripeAmountPaid` is never written by a browser, so its presence is the rail."*
- **Gates (two pinned `git archive` trees, own `npm ci` each, deliberately under SEPARATE parents):** A = `5be583c` (= live), B = `daf1244`. `tsc --noEmit` **0 / 0** · `eslint src` **6 / 6**, no delta; **0** on the six B2a files · full `vitest` **5619 passed / 5679 passed**, **0 failed test cases on both** · skips **6 / 6, identical set** (4 `rules-authority > sibling repos`, 2 `verifyReleaseManifest` — all environment-driven, none a B2a test, none counted as a pass) · one suite fails to load on **both**, `scripts/functionsArchiveManifest.test.js` (`git ls-files` in a non-git archive copy) · `vite build` OK both. B − A = **+60 tests**, exactly the three B2a suites (47 B2a + 13 F2).
- **Negative controls, both directions.** Reverting **only** the F2 source line in the gate workspace turns **exactly five** tests red and leaves 52 green; restoring it returns 57/57 — so the tests bite rather than describe. Eight more add one piece of webhook evidence at a time to the abandoned shape (amount alone, intent alone, ledger record alone, a full capture) and require the block back, including the **2026-09-10 census shape** (20 of 25 exposed DEPOSIT bookings hold a webhook-written `stripeAmountPaid` with **no** `paymentProvider`), so the fix can never hide money.
- **Pre-upload gate.** The baseline tree rebuilt the live bundle **byte-identically (33/33 text assets)** in the same session, so the comparison is like-for-like. The candidate build then changed **exactly one chunk's content** — the entry chunk, `1,291,812 → 1,299,735 B`, **+7,923 B** — with 26 chunks differing only by the renamed entry-import path and the CSS byte-identical. The deploy build reproduced the gate build exactly, down to the entry filename `index-BOx4e_RG.js`.
- **Release:** `firebase deploy --only hosting:salown --project havuz-44f70` from a clean `git archive daf1244` workspace with its own `npm ci`, 2026-09-22 15:43 UK. Pre-deploy rollback identity recorded first. New live **version `8ad0529e5e7be72e`**, release time **2026-09-22T14:43:41.264Z**. The CLI's `found 95 files in hosting` is the **local** count; the published version is **97**, same as before — Firebase adds `/__/firebase/init.js` and `init.json`. Published file diff against the previous version: **27 assets renamed 1:1, 0 added, 0 removed.**
- **Post-release verification (read-only, production).** `/` `/app` `/book/**` `/login` all **200 `text/html`**; `/staff-bundle/index.html` still **302 → staff.salown.com**, so the `R-2026-09-20-A` shadow fix is intact. The served `/app` document references `assets/index-BOx4e_RG.js` — **read from the HTML, not guessed** — and **55 of 56 served text assets are byte-identical** to the pinned build (the 56th is Firebase's auto-injected `init.js`, which has no local counterpart). Markers in the **downloaded** bundle: `online-payment-fees`, `Online payment · Stripe`, `Awaiting Stripe`, `Remaining fee cost`, `Known fee cost`, `Estimated fee cost`, `Charged more than once`, `Paid before fee tracking began`, `fees-net`, `fees-cost`, `settledNetStatus`, `refundsComplete` — **all present, all absent from the previous live bundle**, which was checked the same way before the deploy. **F2 proven in the served bytes**, not merely in source: the minified reader's evidence test reads `if(!(n||r||i>0||eR(t.stripePaymentIntent)!==``))` — the `EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT` comparison is gone and the Connect branch beside it is unchanged.
- **Authenticated UI pass (owner's own browser session, read-only, no click that writes).** ① Booking `jgyXnVAcMkQxdiNfsctO` (**James**, The Full Experience, 22 Sep 18:30, Website) renders **`Paid online +£40.00` · `Stripe fee — Confirmed by Stripe — −£0.80` · `Net from this payment — After Stripe fees and refunds · not yet matched to a payout — £39.20`** — the first end-to-end proof that the ledger and the display agree on a real payment. ② Abandoned checkout `bjku8ZxN…` (**Kostas**, Clipper Cut, 10 Sep, CANCELLED, Website) shows **no `Online payment · Stripe` section at all** — F2, live. ③ **Live negative control:** a real historical Website payment (**Conor Tiernan**, 16 Sep, £40.00) **still** shows the block — `Paid online +£40.00` · `Stripe fee — There is no Stripe fee record for this payment — Not recorded` · `Net from this payment — up to £40.00` — so F2 did not over-hide. **0 console errors.**
- **Negative controls held.** `hosting:salown-staff` still **`e0fab0365adaca37`** (2026-09-21T22:46:26.349Z) · ruleset still **`5e102dd4-…`** to the microsecond · **3** composite indexes, all READY, ids unchanged · `settlementLedgerEnabled: true` and `autoRefundEnabled: true`, settings `updateTime` still **2026-09-22T11:13:40.640511Z** — **`WC_SETTLEMENT_START_ISO` and the whole `R-2026-09-22-A` release are untouched** · Cloud Run **31** us-central1 / **94** europe-west2, `stripewebhook-00107-yus` and `wcsettlementsweeper-00001-cat` unchanged · `origin/main` still `55ec9df` · the shared working checkout never used for the build and left clean on another session's branch. No production document written.
- **Rollback:** re-release `hosting:salown` version **`be573ea0498fc71f`** (release `1789909611896000`, 2026-09-20T13:06:51.896Z, 97 files) — Console → Hosting → site `salown` → Release history → ⋮ → Roll back. This is frontend display code on a hosting-only release: nothing was migrated and nothing is written by it, so rolling back takes the fee block off the screen and changes nothing else. **The settlement ledger keeps recording either way** — B2a only decides whether the owner can see it.
- **Known exclusions / follow-ups.** **F3** — 30 of the 31 tracked bookings predate `WC_SETTLEMENT_START_ISO` and carry no `settlementSync` marker, so they read *"There is no Stripe fee record for this payment"* rather than *"Paid before fee tracking began"*; firing the kinder copy on date alone would put a policy date inside a deliberately policy-free reader, so it is an open design question. **F5** — a genuine £0 actual fee renders `−£0.00 · Confirmed by Stripe`, correct and distinguishable from unknown, but no test pins it. Both are in `FIN_B2A_RELEASE_PREFLIGHT.md` §11. Also still out: B2 P&L / Bank Balance / fee-day grouping, B3 backfill, the no-checkout fee date, the open-period refund day, and the T18 write-once first-checkout field.

## R-2026-09-23-B — the calendar told a leaver he had not started yet · 1 unit (`hosting:salown`) · **LIVE_VERIFIED**

- **Why, and it was seen on the real grid.** The evening `R-2026-09-23-A` shipped, the owner opened Tuesday 29 September and found Muhamed's column badged **"Before start date · record only"**. His `availabilityFrom` is `2026-06-09` — four months past. The badge was not vague, it was false.
- **Cause, one line, and it was mine.** `getEffectiveStaffShift` asks the window through the one predicate, and `STAFF-AVAILABILITY-UNTIL` had just given that predicate a SECOND bound while leaving it a single refusal name: `if (!isWithinAvailabilityWindow(staff, dateKey)) return off('not-started')`. The calendar chooses its lane from that reason code (`bh.reason === 'not-started'` → the pre-start badge), so the day a cutoff took effect the grid began announcing that the person had not started. Two bounds, one name.
- **Fix:** the decision stays with the predicate — there is no second rule — but the refusal is NAMED for the bound that produced it. A new `after-last-day` reason sits beside `not-started`, and the grid gets a third lane from it.
- **The lane is the one the owner specified, and it matches what already existed rather than inventing a fourth behaviour.** VISIBLE, not hidden (they are still `active`, the departure is unrecorded, and their existing future bookings must stay on screen) · DIMMED and UNCLICKABLE (`isDimLane` and `isOutsideHours` both take the flag, at BOTH grid sites; `bh.isOff` was already true, so this is belt and braces exactly as the pre-start lane has it) · LABELLED TRUTHFULLY — **"Last available day: 27 Sep · no new bookings"** / **"Son uygun gün: 27 Eyl · yeni randevu yok"**, with the date built from the key's own PARTS because `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')` parses as UTC and would announce the wrong last working day west of it.
- **Once `OFFBOARD` is recorded none of this applies:** `isInactiveStaff` wins first and the `STAFF-OFFBOARD-TERMINAL` behaviour takes over unchanged — hidden on a clean future day, *"Inactive · needs reassignment"* when unresolved bookings remain, records kept on historical days. That arm was not touched.
- **Source:** salown-app **`37506d6`**, branch `fix/after-last-day-lane`, on `6c55dc2` (the outgoing live source). `origin/main` not merged, unchanged at **`a85bcf1`**.
- **Scope — six files:** `utils/staffAvailability.ts` +11/−2 · `components/TimeGrid.tsx` +33/−3 · both `panel.ts` dictionaries +1 each · `availabilityWindow.test.ts` +90 · `passiveAuthority.test.ts` +5/−1. **No Functions, no rules, no `firebase.json`, no CI, no migration, no backfill, no production write.** B2a/F2's three files do not appear in the diff.
- **`passiveAuthority.test.ts`'s middle-rung pin was updated to the new shape and NOT weakened** — it still asserts the window gate sits between `passive` and the dated override, which is the contract it exists for.
- **Gates on `37506d6`:** `tsc` clean both projects · frontend **5739 pass / 2 fail** (the machine-state negative control) · functions **2681 pass / 0 fail / 44 skip** (untouched; `57aba20`'s emulator **645/645** still applies) · Admin and Staff builds clean. Eleven new tests pin the reason split (both bounds, malformed, and `passive` still outranking), the lane's flag at both sites, dim + unclickable, **not hidden**, the badge never reaching the pre-start sentence, and the date built without a parsed `Date`.
- **Two of my own tests were red first** — one assumed a default export from a named-export dictionary, one imported with a `.ts` extension, which **`tsc` caught and vitest had swallowed**. The tests were fixed; the code was not bent to fit them.
- **Release:** `npm run deploy:panel` from the isolated `37506d6` clone. `2cb2d07dd8a3b104` → **`102a5b6d0504adf5`** (release `1790122855154000`, 2026-09-23T00:20:55.154Z). Only `hosting[salown]` ran.
- **Post-release verification from the SERVED bytes.** `/app` → `index-CaRkTTAn.js` → `Barbers-DcJGPnkW.js`, both **byte-identical** to the local build, and the grid's own chunk `Dashboard-Ey6wMpLB.js` byte-identical too. The live minified grid reads `o = !i && D[t]?.reason === \`after-last-day\``, `c = i || a || o` and `onClick: t => { p ? p(e) : c || b({…}) }` — so the lane is flagged, dimmed and its slot popup is blocked IN PRODUCTION — and the badge renders `g('dashboard.grid.afterLastDayLane', {date: s})`. The resolver half reads `if(!dA(e,r)) return CL(uA(lA(e,r)) ? \`after-last-day\` : \`not-started\`)`.
- **A counting trap avoided:** `after-last-day` appears only ONCE in each chunk, because the bundler hoists repeated literals. The count proves nothing on its own, so the expressions were read rather than tallied — the same discipline applied to `EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT` below.
- **B2a/F2 proven intact in the live minified bytes:** `if(!(n||r||i>0||iR(t.stripePaymentIntent)!==\`\`))return{...sR}` — no `EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT` in the expression.
- **Negative controls held.** `hosting:salown-staff` still `e0fab0365adaca37` · ruleset still `5e102dd4-…` (2026-09-10T13:39:16.896425Z) · `salownstafflifecycle` still **`-00004-saz`** at 100%, no Functions deploy · Cloud Run **94**/**31** · `/staff-bundle/index.html` still **302** · `origin/main` `a85bcf1` · **and Muhamed's document re-read after the deploy: `status: active`, `availabilityFrom: 2026-06-09`, `availabilityUntil: 2026-09-27` — unchanged, no production write.**
- **Rollback:** re-release `hosting:salown` version **`2cb2d07dd8a3b104`** (release `1790122855154000`'s predecessor, 2026-09-22T23:46:05.122Z). It restores `R-2026-09-23-A` exactly — the lane goes back to being dimmed and unclickable but labelled "Before start date".
- **Still owed:** the departure. `OFFBOARD` on or after 2026-09-27 closes the pay period and the rota, and will refuse with `BOOKINGS_AFTER_LAST_WORKING_DAY` if unresolved bookings remain after that Sunday.

## OWNER WRITE 2026-09-23 — `whitecross` / Muhamed `availabilityUntil = 2026-09-27` (no release)

Recorded here because it is a production data change, not a deploy, and it was made by the OWNER from the panel rather than by an agent.

- **What:** `tenants/whitecross/barbers/barber-1781007454543` gained `availabilityUntil: '2026-09-27'`, through the Team Members drawer → *Last available day*, i.e. `salownStaffLifecycle` `op: SET_AVAILABILITY_UNTIL` — owner-gated, precondition-checked, audited.
- **Read-only confirmation (2026-09-23):** `name: Muhamed · status: active · active: true · availabilityFrom: 2026-06-09 · availabilityUntil: 2026-09-27`. Alex and Arda unchanged (`Arda` still `passive` from `R-2026-08-30-B`).
- **Effect:** Muhamed works and is bookable through Sunday 2026-09-27; from Monday 2026-09-28, tenant-local, no surface offers him for NEW work. Nothing else moved — status is still `active`, no `staffComp` period was closed, no rota entry written, no account touched, no existing booking altered.
- **Still owed:** the departure itself. `OFFBOARD` on or after 2026-09-27 closes the pay period and the rota. It will refuse with `BOOKINGS_AFTER_LAST_WORKING_DAY` if unresolved bookings remain after that Sunday.
- **Reversible:** the panel offers *Cancel scheduled departure* until the 27th and *Lift leaving date* afterwards; both clear `availabilityUntil` alone through the same audited call.
- **No agent write.** This entry exists so the record is not mistaken for one.

## R-2026-09-23-A — `STAFF-AVAILABILITY-UNTIL` UX repair: the scheduled departure is reachable, and an in-force one is liftable · 1 unit (`hosting:salown`) · **LIVE_VERIFIED**

- **Why:** `R-2026-09-22-D` shipped the control and the owner could not find it. Reported as *"you cannot pick a future date"*. The bytes were innocent — a rebuild of `57aba20` reproduced **31/31 live assets byte-for-byte**, and the cutoff's own picker carried `min` with **no** `max`. What the owner hit was the DEPARTURE sheet, reached by pressing `Passive`, which is the word you press when somebody is leaving, and whose picker caps at today. That cap is correct and is untouched: `OFFBOARD` writes an undated `status: 'passive'`, so a future departure would make somebody unbookable on days they are still working. The defect was that the refusal named no alternative, and the explanation of the refusal sat UNDERNEATH a field no picker will open past today — unreachable by everyone it was written for.
- **Second defect, found by the owner asking the right question.** The first fix withheld every action once the cutoff took effect, reasoning that putting somebody back on the calendar is not the same decision as calling off a plan. The reasoning is sound; the locked door was not. **This repo had already settled it in writing, for the rota engine:** `ROTA_SUPERSEDE` is *"DELIBERATELY NOT GATED"*, because gating a withdrawal *"would be actively harmful … neither activated NOR withdrawn, which is the one state with no way out"*. A leaving date typed onto the wrong day is exactly that state. **And the server never agreed with the door either** — its past-date refusal reads `!clearing && (availabilityUntil as string) < todayKey`, so a clear is accepted at any date, cutoff passed or not. The earlier note in `R-2026-09-22-D` that *"closing that would be a Functions change"* was **wrong**: nothing needed closing, and no Functions or rules change is made here.
- **Source:** salown-app **`6c55dc2`**, branch `fix/availability-until-lift`, on the live Admin source **`57aba20`** (`6a1d204` superseded). `origin/main` was **not** merged and is unchanged at **`a85bcf1`** — it still carries neither B2a nor this feature, deliberately.
- **Scope — two files, `src/pages/Barbers.tsx` +137/−9 and `src/utils/staffLifecycleIntent.test.ts` +142.** Frontend only.
- **What the panel does now.** ① In the departure sheet, ABOVE the capped field: one sentence and an owner-gated **"Set a last available day instead…"** that opens the cutoff control with tomorrow pre-filled, derived from the TENANT's day, writing nothing itself. ② Still scheduled (`todayKey <= stored`) → **"Cancel scheduled departure"**, one click. ③ Already in force → **"Lift leaving date…"** → an explicit confirmation stating the consequence (*back on the calendar from today*, bookable again immediately, nothing else changed) with **"Yes — remove the availability cutoff"** and a **"Keep it"** to back out. The date field stays shut while a cutoff is in force, because re-dating it later is the same reversal wearing another name — but the reversal sits two lines below, so nothing is stranded.
- **Both actions clear `availabilityUntil` alone**, through the one existing precondition-guarded, audited call. No status, no `staffComp`, no rota, no Auth, no booking, no settlement. No force flag, no booking bypass. Owner/super-admin is re-asserted INSIDE `submitAvailabilityUntil` rather than trusted from the JSX that rendered the button, and the confirmation state resets with the drawer so it cannot leak between members.
- **Gates on `6c55dc2`:** `tsc` clean both projects · frontend **5728 pass / 2 fail** (the machine-state negative control, identical at the base) · functions **2681 pass / 0 fail / 44 skip** — untouched, so `57aba20`'s canonical emulator **645/645** still applies and was deliberately not re-run · Admin and Staff builds clean. Seven new/updated UI contract tests pin: the departure cap still present, the cutoff picker still uncapped, the route-out owner-gated and write-free, `inForce` decided from the tenant day, the lift needing two steps, the clear touching only `availabilityUntil`, and no force/bypass token anywhere in the page.
- **Release:** `npm run deploy:panel` (`vite build && firebase deploy --only hosting:salown --project havuz-44f70`) from the isolated `6c55dc2` clone. `b6a9c8f40bafe558` → **`2cb2d07dd8a3b104`** (release `1790120765122000`, 2026-09-22T23:46:05.122Z = 00:46 UK on 23 Sep), 95 files, 28 uploaded. Only `hosting[salown]` ran.
- **Post-release verification from the SERVED bytes.** `/app` resolves `index-DztsuMXo.js` → `Barbers-xP5ct0_q.js`; both **byte-identical** to the local build. All six new strings present in the served chunk (`Set a last available day instead`, `Cancel scheduled departure`, `Lift leaving date`, `remove the availability cutoff`, `Keep it`, `back on the calendar`) plus `Last available day`, with the control marker `availabilityFrom` ×2 proving the grep reaches the right file.
- **B2a/F2 proven intact IN THE LIVE MINIFIED BYTES**, read rather than counted: `if(!(n||r||i>0||iR(t.stripePaymentIntent)!==``))return{...sR}` — **no `EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT` in the expression**.
- **Negative controls held.** `hosting:salown-staff` still **`e0fab0365adaca37`** (2026-09-21T22:46:26.349Z) · ruleset still **`5e102dd4-e7e7-4950-b12a-14a74daa82e8`** (2026-09-10T13:39:16.896425Z) · `salownstafflifecycle` still **`-00004-saz`** at 100% (no Functions deploy) · Cloud Run **94** europe-west2 / **31** us-central1 · `/staff-bundle/index.html` still **302** to staff.salown.com · `origin/main` **`a85bcf1`** · **no production document written; Muhammed's record is untouched.**
- **Artefact delta vs the outgoing version is 27 of 31 chunks, and that is hash CASCADE rather than scope** — proven, not assumed: the build is deterministic (two builds of one source agree) and rebuilding the unmodified `57aba20` reproduces the previous live assets 31/31.
- **Rollback:** re-release `hosting:salown` version **`b6a9c8f40bafe558`** (release `1790106543165000`, 2026-09-22T19:49:03.165Z) — Console → Hosting → site `salown` → that version → Rollback. It restores `R-2026-09-22-D` exactly: the control returns to its un-discoverable form and the in-force cutoff becomes unliftable from the panel again. Rolling back further to `8ad0529e5e7be72e` removes the feature entirely and keeps B2a/F2.
- **Known exclusion:** `SYNC.md` lives in `salown-app`; recording there needs a push to `main`, which the owner forbade for this work. This row and `DEPLOYMENT_STATUS.md` carry the record.
- **Next:** Muhammed's `availabilityUntil = 2026-09-27` is a SEPARATE owner approval and has not been written. Two facts to carry into it: the cutoff does not close the pay period, so an `OFFBOARD` is still owed on or after that Sunday; and if unresolved bookings sit after it, that `OFFBOARD` will refuse with `BOOKINGS_AFTER_LAST_WORKING_DAY`.

## R-2026-09-22-D — `STAFF-AVAILABILITY-UNTIL` hosting half: the owner control reaches the Admin panel · 1 unit (`hosting:salown`) · **LIVE_VERIFIED**

- **Why:** the Functions half (`R-2026-09-22-C`) made `SET_AVAILABILITY_UNTIL` answerable; this is the only door to it. Team Members → a member → **Last available day**.
- **Owner approval (2026-09-22), and the decision that shaped this release:** deployment **path B** — do **NOT** build the Admin site from `origin/main`. `main` does not contain the live B2a/F2 source, so a main-built release would have reverted the fix shipped six hours earlier in `R-2026-09-22-B`: `settlementFacts.ts` would count `paymentProvider === 'EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT'` as a Stripe payment again, and an ABANDONED checkout would show an online-payment block for money nobody paid.
- **Source:** salown-app **`57aba20`**, branch `release/admin-b2a-availability-until`, cut from the LIVE Admin source **`daf1244`** (= the source of the outgoing version `8ad0529e5e7be72e`). `origin/main` was **not** merged and is unchanged at **`a85bcf1`**.
- **Scope — eight files, +751/−21.** Six are the shipped frontend (`Barbers.tsx`, `staffLifecycleIntent.ts` + test, `availabilityWindow.ts` + test, `staffLifecycleActions.ts`). Two are `functions/` files that **do not ship** — `utils/availabilityWindow.ts` and `bookings/staffEligibility.ts` — present only because the frontend suite BYTE-COMPARES the parity block against the first and calls the second's gates directly; a hosting release publishes `hosting/` alone.
- **How the eight were applied, and why that matters.** Seven had a base IDENTICAL between `daf1244` and pre-feature `main`, so they were taken verbatim from `c8986f0` and carry only this feature. `staffEligibility.ts` was the exception — that base predates STAFF-AVAIL-GAP-P2 — so the same edits were applied onto THIS base and the historical exemption is written the way this base writes it. **`historicalExemptionAllowed` was NOT introduced**; it appears once, in a comment saying so.
- **Deliberately excluded, each verified absent:** Reports passive-barber (`Reports.tsx` +28, `reportsPassiveBarber.test.ts` +51) · the Staff override copy (both `staffApp` dictionaries, +27 each) · `bookingCallables.ts` (+114) · `a2ServiceIdentity.test.ts` · STAFF-AVAIL-GAP-P2's 39 test lines (carrying that file would have imported main work AND gone red here) · **this feature's entire server half**.
- **The PACKAGE carries no server code and no rules.** Zero occurrences under `hosting/` of `setAvailabilityUntilCore`, `lifecycleAvailabilityUntil`, `priceEditExecutor`, `LIFECYCLE_AVAILABILITY_ROLES`, `assertAssignableStaff`, `rescheduleStaffGate`; no `firestore.rules`, no `firebase.json`, no `.ts`. `firestore.rules` is byte-identical to main AND untouched by this release.
- **Gates on `57aba20`** (isolated clone, own `npm ci`): canonical emulator **645/645 PASS** — that is `daf1244`'s own baseline, since this release adds no emulator test file, and the two changed `functions/` modules are exercised by the existing `createWalkIn` / `reassignBooking` / `productSaleCore` / `actorAccessGate` suites, so the green run is real coverage · functions **2681 pass / 0 fail / 44 skip** · frontend **5713 pass / 2 fail** · this feature's own four suites **177/177** · `tsc` clean both projects · Admin and Staff builds clean.

## R-2026-09-22-C — `STAFF-AVAILABILITY-UNTIL` Functions half: a departure can finally be dated forward · 1 unit (`functions:salown` 1 function) · **LIVE_VERIFIED**

- **Why:** `OFFBOARD` refuses a FUTURE `lastWorkingDay` **by name**, and correctly — it writes `status: 'passive'`, which is undated and absolute, so recording Sunday's departure on Tuesday would make the member unbookable on the very days it simultaneously claims they work. An owner told on Tuesday that somebody leaves on Sunday therefore had nowhere to write it down, and the booking page kept selling that person's Monday. `SET_AVAILABILITY_UNTIL` writes that missing fact and **no employment authority**: no status, no `staffComp`, no rota entry, no app access.
- **Source:** salown-app **`a82b5bd`**, branch `integ/staff-availability-until`. Built by `revert a85bcf1` — the inverse of the inverse — because a plain merge could not work: the feature commits are ANCESTORS of `main` (pushed there by accident, then reverted), so `git merge` answers *"Already up to date"* and restores nothing. Proven: `tree(a82b5bd) == tree(c8986f0)` over the feature paths, and the revert had touched only `src/` and `functions/src/`.
- **Scope — twenty files, +2042/−35.** New `functions/src/staff/lifecycleAvailabilityUntil.ts` (the core), the contract op + roles + reason codes, the callable dispatch, both parity copies of `availabilityWindow.ts`, both server gates in `staffEligibility.ts`, the reschedule branch in `index.ts`, the booking-edit executor, and the frontend half.
- **Three real defects found and fixed on the way.** ① `assertAssignableStaff` handed the window predicate a synthetic `{ availabilityFrom }` subject — the server would have kept selling a leaver's Mondays while every browser surface refused them, and **no behavioural test would have failed**, because the frontend passes the real document. ② The CAS was first written as a required `expectedUpdateTime`, which the **Firestore web SDK does not expose** — that would have made the op reachable only by a server caller; the required precondition is now the SEMANTIC one (the value the sheet read), with `updateTime` optional. ③ The **booking-edit executor** (`salownEditBookingForm`, which can MOVE a booking) checked only `passive` — a fourth hand-mirrored copy that had already drifted past `availabilityFrom`, so an operator could drag an appointment onto a date before the assignee started.
- **Gates on `a82b5bd`** (isolated clone, own `npm ci`): canonical emulator **732/732 PASS**, and the base SHA's own run gives **725**, so the delta is **exactly +7** = this feature's emulator suite — which is how we know it RAN rather than self-skipped, the phase logs being deleted with their temp dir · functions **2915 pass / 0 fail / 48 skip** · frontend **5744 pass / 2 fail** (same machine-state control) · `tsc` clean both projects · Admin and Staff builds clean · eslint **source** delta **+6**, all `no-undef require/__dirname` in the two new CJS test files, the pattern every functions test file already carries.
- **Release:** `./scripts/deploy-functions.sh salownStaffLifecycle` (the guarded wrapper — no blanket, no second selector) from the isolated `a82b5bd` clone, 2026-09-22T19:40:55Z. `salownstafflifecycle-00003-bij` → **`salownstafflifecycle-00004-saz`**, 100% traffic.
- **Post-release verification of the ARTIFACT, as the wrapper itself demands.** The deployed `function-source.zip` (generation `1790105988607138`) was downloaded from `gs://gcf-v2-sources-1050766582653-europe-west2` and read: `IMPLEMENTED_OPS` = `[LEAVE_START, LEAVE_END, OFFBOARD, REHIRE, SET_AVAILABILITY_UNTIL]`, `LIFECYCLE_AVAILABILITY_ROLES` = `["owner"]`, all four new reason codes present, `lib/staff/lifecycleAvailabilityUntil.js` shipped (17,758 bytes). **Six `lib/` files byte-identical to the local build**: `lifecycleAvailabilityUntil.js`, `lifecycleContract.js`, `lifecycleCallable.js`, `staffEligibility.js`, `availabilityWindow.js`, `priceEditExecutor.js`.
- **Blast radius = 1.** Exactly one function carries today's deploy stamp (`salownStaffLifecycle`, 19:40:55Z). Cloud Run **94** europe-west2 / **31** us-central1 — ⚠️ the wrapper's printed checklist still says *"the 27 legacy us-central1 functions"*; the real count is **31** and has been for some time. The note is stale, not a finding of this release: no other function was touched.
- **Negative controls held.** Ruleset unchanged · `hosting:salown` still `8ad0529e5e7be72e` at this point · `origin/main` **`a85bcf1`**, not merged and not pushed to · **no production document written.**
- **Rollback:** `gcloud run services update-traffic salownstafflifecycle --region=europe-west2 --to-revisions=salownstafflifecycle-00003-bij=100 --project=havuz-44f70`. Permanent rollback = redeploy the pinned previous source through the same guarded wrapper. Reverting the implementation commit removes the op and returns `OP_NOT_IMPLEMENTED`; nothing already written changes.
- **Ordering was not optional and was observed.** Functions first, hosting second (`R-2026-09-22-D`). Hosting-first would have published a control the server answers `OP_NOT_IMPLEMENTED` to.

## R-2026-09-22-A — `FIN-B1 + FIN-B1b`: the settlement ledger starts recording Stripe captures, fees and refunds · 4-unit release (1 Firestore index · `functions:whitecross` 2 functions · 2 flag writes) · **PARTIALLY LIVE_VERIFIED** — the sweeper leg is verified in production; the capture leg waits for the first real website payment

- **Why:** every Whitecross website payment moved money through Stripe and the platform recorded only the gross. The actual processor fee — the `balance_transaction` figure Stripe already computes — was never stored, so no Finance surface could ever be net of fees. B1 records captures and their actual fees; B1b records refunds, their fees, and the compensation entries for refunds that later fail or cancel. `PROCESSOR_FEES_PLAN.md`, `FIN_B1_RELEASE_PREFLIGHT.md`, `FIN_B1B_RELEASE_PREFLIGHT.md`.
- **Owner approval (2026-09-22), six decisions taken explicitly:** ① Stripe `events.list` + `refunds.list` read-only use approved ② `activateSettlementLedger.mjs` is the sanctioned arm path, dry run first ③ the §5 rollback plan approved as written ④ the full staging rebuild **skipped** in favour of a flag-off inert deploy plus observation ⑤ `autoRefundEnabled` temporarily disarmed across the arm, then re-armed ⑥ `WC_SETTLEMENT_START_ISO` taken immediately before the Functions deploy. Approved sequence: rules-authority 30/30 → index deploy → index READY → flag-off Functions deploy → read-only post-deploy verification → arm. **`charge.updated` was deliberately NOT added to the Stripe endpoint.**
- **Source:** whitecross-site **`925debde`** (`FIN-B1B-RECENCY`) — a source superset of the pinned B1 candidate `22850996`, which was not modified. `functions/settlements.js` sha256 `61e1ebb4764eb9839fd54690beb924a7a5bc5cc7acc2190128a4105397e7c0e9`; its `functions/` tree is an empty diff against `origin/main` (`7775d2cf`). Index from salown-app `origin/main` **`f928117`**, `firestore.indexes.json` sha256 `3498d03dc1ef241ba622721614000276aa2c34db43c68364d7f23bfb3997e88d` — byte-identical to `9a9547a` and `b6c325c`.
- **Delta over the live bundle (`6817356f`), runtime files only:** `index.js` (the B1 branch, the B1b BL-5 hook, the `wcSettlementSweeper` definition), `settlements.js` (new), and **`loyaltyEnroll.js` — the already-live loyalty lane, republished into the `stripeWebhook` bundle as a side effect of this deploy.** `externalCheckout.js`, `refunds.js` and `emailParsers.js` are unchanged bytes, so checkout and refund behaviour is byte-identical.
- **Gates, same day, all from pinned trees:** B1b unit at `925debde` **220/220, 0 skipped** — run in a clone WITH git history and a sibling `salown-app`, so `B1 parity` and the live `readProjection` reader contract both actually executed (in a `git archive` workspace the same command silently degrades to 219/220 + 1 skip; `FIN_B1B_RELEASE_PREFLIGHT.md` §3) · B1 unit at `22850996` **182/182** · fence rehearsal **7/7** · API-read rehearsal **12 steps, 52/52, 291 Stripe calls all reads, 0 write attempts** · arming tool **29/29** · salown-app workspace guards **126/126** · `ops/rules-authority.test.js` **30/30** in the shared checkout immediately before the index step.
- **Unit 1 — Firestore index.** `firebase deploy --only firestore:indexes --project havuz-44f70 --non-interactive` from a `git archive f928117` workspace (`.firebaserc` copied in, no `.git`), after a clean `--dry-run`. Result: **2 → 3 composite indexes, 0 deleted, no deletion prompt.** New index `bookings (settlementSync.state ASC, settlementSync.nextAttemptAt ASC)` COLLECTION, id **`CICAgJjF9oIK`**, `CREATING` → **`READY` at 2026-09-22T00:28:23Z**. The `stripePaymentIntent` field override was already live on both scopes and matched the file, which is why nothing was offered for deletion. **Firestore rules were not released:** ruleset stayed `5e102dd4-e7e7-4950-b12a-14a74daa82e8` (2026-09-10T13:39:16Z) before and after — the CLI compiles `firestore.rules` during an indexes deploy but does not publish it.
- **Unit 2 — `WC_SETTLEMENT_START_ISO` = `2026-09-22T00:29:26Z`.** One UTC instant, taken immediately before the Functions deploy, written into `functions/.env.havuz-44f70` **in the archive workspace only**, together with `WC_STRIPE_ACCOUNT_ID=acct_1T3CrpRfgDnpYJzP` and `WC_STRIPE_LIVEMODE=true`, no `WC_NONPROD_TEST_MODE` line. Verified on the live function after deploy. **This is the ledger's permanent origin and must never move on a redeploy** — backwards re-scans history, forwards creates a permanent blind span. It was a first write: the live function carried no `WC_*` settlement variable beforehand.
- **Unit 3 — Functions.** `bash scripts/deploy-functions.sh whitecross stripeWebhook wcSettlementSweeper` from a `git archive 925debde` workspace. Wrapper guards all passed: rules-authority ✔, `.firebaserc` project ✔, no shared `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` ✔, no salown-owned export ✔, live-key guard **pre and post** ✔ (`Stripe mode = LIVE (Whitecross account confirmed)`). Over that whole window `tenants/whitecross/platform/settlementScan` stayed **404** and the `settlements` collection group stayed **empty**. No confirmation, refund or checkout behaviour changed.
- **Unit 4a — `autoRefundEnabled` temporarily disarmed.** `node scripts/activateAutoRefund.mjs --disarm --expect-update-time 2026-09-20T22:36:13.826815Z` → `true → false`, exactly one field, settings `updateTime 2026-09-20T22:36:13.826815Z → 2026-09-22T06:33:44.502111Z`, audit row `tenants/whitecross/auditLogs/refundflag_1790058824422`. **During this window a customer cancelling outside the 8 h policy window received no automatic refund** and would have had to be handled manually. Result: `<absent> → true`, **exactly one field changed and it is a real boolean**, settings `updateTime → 2026-09-22T11:06:54.560159Z`, audit row `tenants/whitecross/auditLogs/settlementflag_1790075214487` (`SETTLEMENT_LEDGER_ARMED`, `from: null`, `to: true`, `duePassIndexState: READY`, `autoRefundArmedAtWrite: false`, `settingsUpdateTimeBefore: 2026-09-22T06:33:44.502111Z`, `tool: scripts/activateSettlementLedger.mjs`). The flag write and its audit row are one commit carrying an `updateTime` precondition; no Console write was used.
- **Post-arm verification (read-only, production).** First armed pass at **2026-09-22T11:08:08Z**: `enabled: true, configOk: true, due: 0, applied: 0, noop: 0, refused: 0, errors: 0`, `scan: { from: 1790036966, to: 1790058566, pages: 1, pagesCommitted: 1, advanced: true, error: null }` — **`scan.from` decodes to `2026-09-22T00:29:26Z`, exactly `WC_SETTLEMENT_START_ISO`** — and `refundEvents: { windows: 2, pages: 2, advanced: true, error: null, retentionGap: null, refusals: [] }`, so the B1b events backstop ran on its first pass too. `tenants/whitecross/platform/settlementScan` **created** at `11:08:06.287927Z` (`scannedUntil: 1790058566`, `refundEvents.cursor: 1790075164`, `retentionGaps: 0`). **Zero** `ACCOUNT_MISMATCH`, **zero** `CONFIG_MISSING`, **zero** WARNING-or-above log lines on either service since the arm.
- **Unit 4c — `autoRefundEnabled` re-armed.** `node scripts/activateAutoRefund.mjs --arm --expect-update-time 2026-09-22T11:06:54.560159Z` → `false → true`, exactly one field, settings `updateTime → 2026-09-22T11:13:40.640511Z`, audit row `tenants/whitecross/auditLogs/refundflag_1790075620566`. Both switches are now on.
- **Rollback identity, per unit.** **Stop first, always:** `node scripts/activateSettlementLedger.mjs --disarm --expect-update-time <current>` — every new invocation is inert at once; let in-flight work drain (webhook ≤ its timeout, sweeper ≤ 120 s, schedule every 15 min, so at most one already-running pass can still write) and **verify quiescence by the absence of new `settlements` entries across two further scheduler intervals, not by a timestamp**. Writes that land during the drain are valid ledger facts, not errors. **`stripeWebhook`, fastest:** `gcloud run services update-traffic stripewebhook --region=us-central1 --to-revisions=stripewebhook-00106-dof=100 --project=havuz-44f70` — **`stripewebhook-00106-dof` is still listed and Ready**; it holds only until the next deploy of the function. **`stripeWebhook`, permanent:** redeploy a `git archive` of **`6817356f`** with `bash scripts/deploy-functions.sh whitecross stripeWebhook`, from a workspace **without** the env file. **`wcSettlementSweeper`:** pause `firebase-schedule-wcSettlementSweeper-us-central1`, then `firebase functions:delete wcSettlementSweeper --region us-central1 --project havuz-44f70` — **no earlier revision exists, deletion is the only way back.** **Index:** leave it, it is inert. **Rules:** there is no B1/B1b rules rollback — the `settlementLedgerEnabled` arms shipped inseparably with GTM A3 in ruleset `5e102dd4-…`; they are owner-only and loosen nothing. **Data:** entries already written are correct facts and stay; `settlementRefundState` is coordination metadata and never a money authority.
- **⚠️ Two operational notes for the next release from this repo.** ① **`scripts/deploy-functions.sh` is mode `100644` in git** — the executable bit was never committed. The documented command `./scripts/deploy-functions.sh …` therefore only works in a checkout where someone ran `chmod +x` locally; in a clean `git archive` workspace it fails `permission denied`. This release used `bash scripts/deploy-functions.sh …`, which is the same file and runs every guard. ② **`npm ci` inside the archive workspace's `functions/` is mandatory before the Functions deploy.** The first attempt failed at `Couldn't find firebase-functions package in your source code` during source analysis — firebase-tools needs the dependency tree locally to determine what to deploy, even though `node_modules` is in `firebase.json`'s ignore list and is never uploaded. **Nothing was deployed by that failed attempt**, verified immediately afterwards: `stripewebhook-00106-dof` unchanged, `updateTime` still 2026-08-28, sweeper still absent, us-central1 still 30 functions.
- **Why not fully `LIVE_VERIFIED`.** The sweeper leg **is** verified in production — a real scheduled invocation ran with the real configuration against the real project and advanced its cursor. The capture leg is not: **no real website payment has occurred since the arm**, so no `settlements` entry exists yet and `stripeWebhook[B1] settlement recorded` has never been logged. Promotion needs the first live payment to produce `stripe:ch_…` + `stripe:txn_…` with `feeSource: 'actual'` (or `pending` then `actual` on the next sweeper pass), `projection.gross_m` equal to the charge amount, and T6 `fee_m + providerNet_m === gross_m`. **Forcing it is not allowed** — it would mean taking a real payment.
- **Known exclusions.** No Finance, Reports or booking-panel reader ships here — **B2a (`74922bd`, `9a4925c`) is still `PUSHED_NOT_LIVE`, so nothing in the product displays a fee yet.** No history backfill (B3). No `charge.updated` subscription — the endpoint list is unchanged at `charge.refunded`, `checkout.session.completed`, `refund.updated`, which means a capture whose `balance_transaction` is unset at `checkout.session.completed` has the **indexed due pass as its only fee path**. No date policy: the no-checkout fee date and the open-period refund day remain open owner decisions, and the T18 write-once first-checkout field is still not built. No Stripe test-mode call was ever made; no staging project was rebuilt. No main merge, no hosting release, no rules or other index change, no production booking, loyalty or payment document written.

## R-2026-09-20-B — `STAFF-LOYALTY-RELEASE`: the Staff till gets loyalty redemption, the Welcome Offer, and the two guards it was missing · 1-site release (`hosting:salown-staff`) · **ARTIFACT_VERIFIED** (no real checkout observed through it yet)

- **Why:** `staff.salown.com` was running `staff-BVUJwYTL.js`, whose served bytes greped **0** for both `CHECKOUT_ALREADY_APPLIED` and `LOYALTY_CLIENT_MISMATCH` — so the Staff till had no apply-once guard and no loyalty client-ownership check, and no loyalty UI at all. The apply-once defect is reproducible on that exact source: a harness-only port of the writer suite onto `76e58fe` fails 3/7 with `expected -156 to be 22` (a double-applied sale drives a real balance NEGATIVE), `expected 442 not to be 442` (two concurrent corrections invent 210 points) and `expected undefined to be 1` (no `checkoutRevision`). Recorded in [INCIDENTS.md](INCIDENTS.md) 2026-09-18/19/20.
- **Owner approval (2026-09-20):** deploy `bfb8116` to **`hosting:salown-staff` only**, built in a clean archive workspace with its own `npm ci` and production env. Explicitly excluded: `hosting:salown` (Admin), every Cloud Function, `firestore.rules`, `firestore.indexes.json`, Finance, the `main` merge and any real booking/checkout/loyalty document.
- **Source:** `fix/staff-loyalty-client-mismatch` @ **`bfb8116`** = `602c7b9` + the mismatch fix, where `602c7b9` = `release/staff-loyalty-welcome-on-live-76e58fe` = the LIVE Staff source **`76e58fe`** + `8456b1d` + `60eda8a` + `602c7b9`. Cut from LIVE, deliberately **not** from `main` (main is 163 commits ahead of that fork point and carries Finance, Reports, staff-availability and other unreleased Admin work).
- **What `bfb8116` added over `602c7b9` (2 files):** the `LOYALTY_CLIENT_MISMATCH` refusal was gated on `redeemed > 0 || loyaltyRedeemedValue > 0`, so a **Welcome Offer** — a percentage discount read from the client document's own `welcomeOffer`, spending no points — fell through to normal resolution and wrote the sale against a different client than the one whose offer paid for it. **And the refusal had never fired at all:** the `throw` sat inside the loyalty pre-fetch `try` whose `catch` logs `Loyalty pre-fetch failed (non-critical)` and continues. Measured: the console printed `Loyalty pre-fetch failed (non-critical): LOYALTY_CLIENT_MISMATCH: …` and the checkout **completed**, while the refusal's own message said nothing was charged. Fix = the gate removed (a **missing** document is refused too — "unvouchable" is not "absent") plus a typed `CheckoutLoyaltyClientMismatchError` the `catch` re-raises; every other pre-fetch error stays non-critical, because an unreadable balance must never stop a salon taking money.
- **Gates (isolated pinned trees, `git archive` + own `npm ci`, `functions/lib` built on BOTH sides), base `602c7b9` vs `bfb8116`:** `tsc --noEmit` **0 vs 0, logs identical** · full `npm test` **15 fail / 5602 pass / 2 skip** on both, **failing test names identical** → 0 new failures · emulator gate **22/22** (writer 15/15 + welcome 7/7) vs 14/14 on base · checkout-writer unit gates **166/166** · `build:staff` 0 · Admin `npm run build` **0**, so the shared writer still builds for the panel. The 15 residual failures are the three sibling-repo/workspace scanners (`ops/functions-ownership`, `ops/rules-authority`, `scripts/functionsArchiveManifest`) seeing the session's own A/B trees — identical both sides, none in the checkout path.
- **Negative control (the gate is real, not vacuous):** the 8 new cases against the unmodified `602c7b9` give **3 failed / 12 passed**, the three being exactly the refusal cases; and all **6 PRESERVED controls passed before the fix** (correct id with a redemption, correct id on an offer, no id at all, fully pre-paid, loyalty disabled), so they pin existing behaviour rather than describe new behaviour. The pre-existing ownership test could not see either defect: it called the writer inside `.catch(() => {})` and asserted only that the stranger's balance had not moved — equally true when the whole loyalty block bails out.
- **Pre-deploy live (read-only):** Staff version **`4c0ce013b1e45dfa`**, release `1789679980588000` (2026-09-17T21:19:34.687Z), 27 files / 1,506,792 B, serving `/assets/staff-BVUJwYTL.js`. Admin recorded as `be573ea0498fc71f` / release `1789909611896000` / 97 files / 3,937,077 B. Firestore ruleset `5e102dd4-e7e7-4950-b12a-14a74daa82e8` (updateTime 2026-09-10T13:39:16.896Z), Storage ruleset `4c00eef7-e45c-4b35-856a-b0e911018990`. Functions inventory 124. Indexes 2 composite + 1 field override.
- **⚠️ Method note — status codes are meaningless on this site.** `hosting[salown-staff]` has a catch-all rewrite `**` → `/index.html`, so **every** path returns **200**, including assets that do not exist: a not-yet-deployed filename returned `200 text/html 10678`. Verification is therefore by **content-type + bytes**, never by status. The `10678`-byte `text/html` response is this site's "I hashed the fallback page" sentinel, the equivalent of the Admin site's empty-string hash trap.
- **Release:** `firebase deploy --only hosting:salown-staff --project havuz-44f70` from a clean `git archive bfb8116` workspace (own `npm ci`, production env, no `.secret.local`, no `.git`), 2026-09-20 22:53 UK. CLI enumerated **"found 25 files in hosting/staff-bundle"**, 2 new files uploaded. New live **version `dc3e5984e1b9a63f`**, release **`1789944796029000`** (22:53:09.483Z), API reports 27 files / 1,512,237 B (the API counted 27 for the previous version too, against a 25-file directory both times — a consistent accounting difference, not a file the deploy added).

## R-2026-09-20-A — `SHADOW-STAFF-BUNDLE`: `salown.com/staff-bundle/` stops serving a pre-enforcement Staff app · 1-site release (`hosting:salown`) · **LIVE_VERIFIED**

- **Why:** `https://salown.com/staff-bundle/index.html` returned **200** and loaded `staff-CRqN2rBX.js` — a Staff build OLDER than `STAFF-AVAIL-GAP` Phase 1+2. Callable-name literals (which survive minification) prove it: no `salownCreateStaffBooking`, no `salownCreateStaffWalkIn`, and the legacy unenforced `salownCreateWalkIn` present. A shipped safety control could be bypassed from a URL nobody published and nobody monitored. Not a data leak and not privilege escalation — same auth, same rules. Full record: [SEC_SHADOW_STAFF_BUNDLE.md](SEC_SHADOW_STAFF_BUNDLE.md).
- **⚠️ Correction of record:** the `R-2026-09-19-B` row concluded that the `salown` copy "has no reachable entry point" because `/staff-bundle/staff.html` returns 404. `staff.html` is not the entry point of that build — `/staff-bundle/index.html` is, and it was live. The side effect that row disclosed (both `predeploy` hooks fire regardless of `--only`) was the mechanism that kept rewriting it.
- **Root cause (read from firebase-tools 15.15.0):** `lib/deploy/lifecycleHooks.js` → `getReleventConfigs()` filters hosting configs by `config.target`; both configs here declare `site`, so `!config.target` is always true and **every** hosting deploy runs **both** predeploy hooks — `build:staff` rewrites `hosting/staff-bundle/`. The `salown` site then published it because its `public` is `hosting` and `ignore` did not name it. (`lib/hosting/config.js` → `filterOnly()` scopes the deploy itself correctly by `site`; only the hook dispatcher is target-keyed.)
- **Owner approval (2026-09-20):** deploy `5be583c` to `hosting:salown` **only**, from a clean archive workspace; no main deploy, no merge, no Staff hosting change, no artefact untrack or deletion; Finance, Staff availability, Reports, client identity, Functions, rules and every other hosting target excluded.
- **Source:** `security/shadow-staff-bundle-on-live-56ceccc` @ **`5be583c`** = live Admin source **`56ceccc`** + one commit. Cut from LIVE, deliberately **not** from `main` (main carries FIN-PROCESSOR-FEES B2/B2a, the Staff availability work and the Reports fix as unreleased Admin changes). Diff = 2 files, +105/−1: `firebase.json` (`hosting[salown].ignore` += `staff-bundle/**`; `/staff-bundle` and `/staff-bundle/**` → **302** `https://staff.salown.com/`) and the new `ops/hosting-shadow-bundle.test.js`. **No application byte changes** — the shipped Admin bundle is identical on both sides.
- **Why those exact two lines:** the ignore pattern is resolved against the *publishing site's* `public` root, so `hosting/staff-bundle/**` would ignore nothing and fail silently; the guard therefore derives it as `relative(adminPublic, staffPublic)`. `302`, never `301`: a permanent redirect is cached client-side and would defeat the rollback. Redirect to the Staff site ROOT, not a path-preserving splat — the asset hashes differ between the two bundles.
- **Gates (candidate, isolated clone, own `npm ci`):** publish set via firebase-tools' own `listFiles()` `salown` **120 → 95**, `staff-bundle/` **25 → 0**, `salown-staff` **25 → 25** · hosting emulator: `/staff-bundle/**` **302**, `/` `/app` `/book/**` **200**, Staff site **200** · `npm test` **5619 pass / 16 fail / 2 skipped** vs pristine `56ceccc` **5607 / 16 / 2** — identical failures (sibling-repo scanners), **+12 = the new guard** · negative control on the unpatched base **2 failed / 10 passed**, exactly the ignore rule and the redirect pair · `npm run build` OK, `hosting/staff-bundle` checksum unchanged by it · `ops/deploy-policy.test.js` passes.
- **Pre-deploy live (read-only):** Admin version **`07c90b756c541a6a`**, release `1789843984606000` (2026-09-19T18:53:04.606Z), **122 files / 5,443,903 B**; a `git archive 5be583c` + `npm ci` + `npm run build` comparison against the served bytes was **55/55 identical, 0 differ, 0 missing**, proving live was on `56ceccc` and that this candidate changes no application byte. Staff site recorded as `4c0ce013b1e45dfa` / release `1789679980588000` / 27 files.
- **Release:** `firebase deploy --only hosting:salown --project havuz-44f70` from a clean `git archive 5be583c` workspace (own `npm ci`, no `.git`), 2026-09-20 13:06 UK. CLI enumerated **"found 95 files in hosting"**. New live **version `be573ea0498fc71f`**, release **`1789909611896000`** (13:06:51.896Z), **97 files / 3,937,077 B**.
- **Post-release verification (read-only):** version file count **122 → 97 = −25**, bytes −1,506,826 — exactly the 25-file `staff-bundle/` artefact · `/staff-bundle/`, `/staff-bundle/index.html`, `/staff-bundle/assets/staff-CRqN2rBX.js`, `/staff-bundle/assets/staff-BVUJwYTL.js`, `/staff-bundle/sw.js`, `/staff-bundle/site.webmanifest` all **302 → `https://staff.salown.com/`**, **no 301 anywhere** · `/`, `/app`, `/book/whitecross`, `/login`, `/features` **200** · all **55** Admin bundle files plus `index.html` and `features.html` **byte-identical** to the deployed workspace build.
- **Negative controls:** `hosting:salown-staff` still version **`4c0ce013b1e45dfa`** / release `1789679980588000` / 27 files, and `staff.salown.com` still serves `staff-BVUJwYTL.js`. No function, no ruleset, no index, no other hosting target touched. No production document read or written.
- **Rollback:** redeploy `hosting:salown` from **`56ceccc`** — the source the site was already running — or promote version **`07c90b756c541a6a`** (release `1789843984606000`). The redirect is a `302`, so nothing is cached permanently on any client.
- **⚠️ `main` does NOT carry this fix.** `5be583c` was not merged (owner decision). **An Admin deploy from `main` would republish the shadow bundle**, because `main`'s `firebase.json` still lacks both lines. Cherry-picking it onto `main` is a separate, pending decision — until then this is a standing trap on any future `main`-based release. **✅ CLOSED 2026-09-20 15:15 UK:** the two files were carried onto `main` at **`06dc7e4`** (`git diff 5be583c origin/main -- firebase.json ops/hosting-shadow-bundle.test.js` is empty, i.e. main now holds byte-for-byte what is deployed). Source-only, `[skip ci]`, nothing live changed. **The client-identity half of the trap remains OPEN** — see the next sentences. **Compounded 2026-09-20 14:xx:** `main` now also carries **unreleased, un-approved Admin source** — `CLIENT-IDENTITY-P1` (`a2a627b`, `src/utils/clientIdentity.ts`, `clientTombstone`, `clientWriter`, `Clients.tsx`, `firestoreActions.ts` + `functions/src`), which reached `main` when a claim-only push named a SHA that descended from the code commit. Source-only and gated, never released. So a `main`-based Admin deploy today would republish the shadow bundle **and** ship client-identity Phase 1 — two unreviewed payloads in one release. **✅ RESOLVED 2026-09-20 ~16:00 UK:** the owner chose revert. `a2a627b` is off `main` (revert `5a32334`, main `beb1d61`); its 23 paths are byte-identical to `a2a627b^` again, verified read-only. The shadow-bundle fix was untouched by it — `firebase.json` and `ops/hosting-shadow-bundle.test.js` on `main` are still byte-identical to the deployed `5be583c`, and the guard passes 40/40 with `ops/deploy-policy.test.js` on the post-revert tree. Live never moved: `be573ea0498fc71f`, `index-CiEeRNFs.js`, `/staff-bundle/**` still 302. **Trap for later:** `a2a627b` is an ancestor of `feat/client-identity-phase1`, so a plain merge of that branch will NOT restore the code — it needs `git revert 5a32334` first. **And it is not only a hosting risk:** `a2a627b` also lands 17 files under `functions/src` (`clients/{identity,identityTokens,ensureClient,ensureClientCompat,tombstone,reviewQueue,mergeJournal}`, `parsers/{importClientLink,booksy,fresha,treatwell}` and the two `resolveClientDocId` call sites in `index.ts`) — verified present on `main`. A functions release cuts its archive from the **working tree**, not from a pinned tree (`scripts/deploy-functions.sh` → `firebase deploy --only functions:salown:<fn>`, whose predeploy is `npm --prefix "$RESOURCE_DIR" run build`), so **a single targeted function deploy taken from `main` would ship Phase 1's server code with no hosting release involved at all** — the same shared-module ride-along recorded as M5 in the closing-coordination record. Nothing of it is deployed today: `functions/lib` was only rebuilt locally by an emulator gate. Live is unaffected: version `be573ea0498fc71f` still serves `index-CiEeRNFs.js` with zero Phase 1 markers, and no CI run followed (every commit carried `[skip ci]`).
- **Known exclusions:** main merge · `hosting:salown-staff` · the `site`→`target` predeploy rename (would break `ops/deploy-policy.test.js`) · untracking `hosting/staff-bundle/**` (closes nothing on its own) · FIN-PROCESSOR-FEES B2/B2a · Staff availability (M3) · Reports (M4) · `settlementSync` index · client identity work · every Cloud Function · `firestore.rules`.

## R-2026-09-19-B — `CHECKOUT-TIP-PREPAID` + `ADMIN-CHECKOUT-CONCURRENCY` + `CHECKOUT-REVISION-GUARD` (Admin) · 1-site release (`hosting:salown`) · **TIP HALF `LIVE_VERIFIED` (owner-confirmed on the live panel 2026-09-19) · APPLY-ONCE HALF `ARTIFACT_VERIFIED` only (emulator-proven, deliberately never production-tested)**

- **Why:** three defects in the Admin till, all reached through `checkoutBooking`. (1) The percentage tip buttons took a percentage of the REMAINING BALANCE, so a fully pre-paid booking collapsed 10/15/20/25% to £0.00 — live controls whose only possible answer was zero — and a £10 deposit turned 20% of a £32 visit into £4.40 instead of £6.40. (2) One checkout could be applied TWICE: the booking stamp and the client's unclamped `increment()` were separate writes, so two concurrent callers both applied their delta; measured on a real emulator, two simultaneous £32 checkouts redeeming 200 points left the client on **−156** instead of 22. (3) The first fix's discriminator was a TIMESTAMP, which cannot separate two applications inside one millisecond.
- **Owner approval (2026-09-19):** scope confirmed as "current live + the tip and transaction fix; the other nine commits stay out of scope", then deploy approved for **`2957fdf` → `hosting:salown` only**, following `docs/2026-09-19-release-plan-2957fdf.md`, with instructions not to re-ask after a clean baseline check. Explicitly excluded by the owner: `firestore.rules`, Cloud Functions, the `hosting:salown-staff` release, and any merge to `main`.
- **Source:** `release/admin-tip-concurrency-on-live-503bdff` @ **`2957fdf`** = live Admin source **`503bdff`** + `53c88fd` (tip base) + `158923f` (one transaction + compare-and-set, swallow-catch removed) + `2957fdf` (monotonic `checkoutRevision` replacing the millisecond time comparison). Cut from LIVE, not from `main`.
- **Why the tip number changes is not a new policy:** `subtotal` is byte-identical between `503bdff` and the candidate (`max(0, startingTotal - discountAmt - pointsApplied + serviceCharge)`), and `resolveTipBase` is that expression with ONE term removed. Money order (discount → points → service charge) and `total = subtotal + tip` are unchanged.
- **New stored field:** `checkoutRevision` (integer), written on every checkout, absent on all pre-existing bookings where it reads as 0. **No migration, no backfill.** Additive: the previous bundle ignores it.
- **Pre-release (read-only, no rules deploy):** live ruleset **`5e102dd4-e7e7-4950-b12a-14a74daa82e8`** fetched and found **byte-identical** to the repo `firestore.rules` (79,060 bytes); the live bookings `allow update` confirms an authenticated till short-circuits on `isTenantAny` BEFORE the anonymous branch's `affectedKeys().hasOnly([...])` allow-list, so the new key cannot be refused for being new. Live hosting had **not drifted**: version `3d090f91a64cea18` / release `1789679753178000` matched the `R-2026-09-17-B` row with nothing newer, and a `git archive 503bdff` + `vite build` comparison against the served bundle was **55/55 identical, 0 differ, 0 missing**, reproducing `index-DYUKBSbN.js` exactly. Baselines re-read immediately before deploying and all unchanged. Claim `f2a97c5`.
- **Gates:** `tsc --noEmit` 0 · five mocked writer suites **113/113** · `scripts/adminCheckoutWriter.emulator.test.ts` **5/5** against a real Firestore+Auth emulator (two concurrent first checkouts · lost-response retry · legitimate re-checkout correction · two concurrent corrections · **two concurrent corrections sharing one frozen millisecond**). Bracketed by two negative controls: removing the counter check makes the frozen-clock case **fail at 442** (232 expected), removing the timestamp check leaves all 5 passing — so the counter is sufficient and the timestamp is not necessary.
- **Release:** `firebase deploy --only hosting:salown --project havuz-44f70 --non-interactive` (pinned CLI **15.26.0**, invoked by absolute path so `npx` could not resolve an unpinned version) from a clean isolated `git archive` workspace with its own `npm ci` and **no `.git`**, 2026-09-19 09:20 UK. New live **version `b6466893d225b0d5`**, release **`1789809623008000`**. Previous: version `3d090f91a64cea18`, release `1789679753178000`.
- **Post-release verification (code, by this session):** `/app` references `/public-bundle/assets/index-DHeEezw7.js` (read from the page, not guessed) → **200**; all **55 served files byte-identical to the deployed workspace build**, 0 differ, 0 missing; the superseded `index-DYUKBSbN.js` now returns **404**, so the new version really is serving; served bundle contains `CHECKOUT_ALREADY_APPLIED` ×1 and `checkoutRevision` ×1 and **0** hits for the old `Client update failed` swallow, `EMULATOR MODE`, `demo-staffloyalty`, `connectFirestoreEmulator`; `https://salown.com/` 200, `/app` 200, `/s/whitecross` 200.
- **Negative controls, re-read after the deploy:** `hosting:salown-staff` version **`4c0ce013b1e45dfa`** / release `1789679980588000` **unchanged**, and `staff.salown.com` still serves `staff-BVUJwYTL.js`. Live Firestore ruleset still `5e102dd4-e7e7-4950-b12a-14a74daa82e8` (updateTime still 2026-09-10T13:39:16Z) — **no rules deploy**. No Cloud Function touched. No production booking created, no checkout or re-checkout executed, no concurrency test run against live data, no tenant data written.
- **⚠️ Measured side effect — REL-1, disclosed:** both `hosting[]` `predeploy` hooks fire regardless of `--only`, so `build:staff` ran and the `salown` site's own COPY of the staff bundle was replaced: version file lists are 122 → 122 with 28 added / 28 removed = 27 `public-bundle` chunk renames (Vite re-hashes the whole graph) **plus `/staff-bundle/assets/staff-CRqN2rBX.js` added and `/staff-bundle/assets/staff-B4PSl4S1.js` removed**. This is NOT a Staff release: the `salown-staff` site did not move. It is also inert — `https://salown.com/staff-bundle/staff.html` returns **404**, so that copy has no reachable entry point. Note the outgoing copy `staff-B4PSl4S1.js` already differed from the live Staff site's asset, i.e. the `salown` copy was divergent BEFORE this release. Because the deploy ran from an ephemeral extracted workspace with no `.git`, no tracked artefact in any real checkout was dirtied and no `git restore` cleanup was needed.
- **Deliberately NOT released:** the **nine Admin-shipped commits on `main`** that this candidate does not contain (`9a4925c`, `74922bd`, `aa2efd9`, `a7b1f33`, `261fed1`, `9ea0aca`, `631b768`, `a7c79f3`, `da44310` — Finance Stripe-fee work, walk-in backdate floor, staff-availability enforcement, reports/toast fixes). `main` remains ahead of live in ways unrelated to this work.
- **Rollback identity:** `hosting:salown` version **`3d090f91a64cea18`** (release `1789679753178000`) via Console → Hosting → site `salown` → Release history → ⋮ → Roll back (`hosting:clone` is not a rollback tool — it takes `siteId:channelId`, not a version id). Then re-read the served asset path and confirm it matches that version's own build rather than assuming. **Data needs no undo** — `checkoutRevision` is additive and the old bundle ignores it — but a rollback restores the double-application defect, which is why the incident stays open until the owner's usage verification passes.
- **Still open after this release:** the Staff App carries the same writer defect in production until the Welcome Offer session's combined candidate (`release/staff-loyalty-welcome-on-live-76e58fe` @ `602c7b9`, which has `8456b1d` as an ancestor and carries the same guard) is released separately — this approval did not release it. Unrelated and unfixed: the Admin till still burns loyalty points on a fully pre-paid checkout with no compensation (INCIDENTS 2026-09-18, 🔴 Open, production reach unmeasured). Observation, not a regression claim: `https://salown.com/book` returns 404; no baseline was taken for that path before the release, so it is recorded as unverified rather than attributed either way.
- **Owner usage verification: DONE for the TIP half, 2026-09-19.** The owner tried the percentage tip buttons on the live panel and confirmed the amounts are now produced correctly; no collection was completed, and none was required for the check. `CHECKOUT-TIP-PREPAID` is therefore `LIVE_VERIFIED`.
- **⚠️ The APPLY-ONCE half is NOT owner-verified, and by design never will be.** `ADMIN-CHECKOUT-CONCURRENCY` and `CHECKOUT-REVISION-GUARD` are live and artefact-verified (the served bundle carries `CHECKOUT_ALREADY_APPLIED` and `checkoutRevision`, and the old swallow is gone), but their BEHAVIOUR is proven only against a local emulator — two concurrent first checkouts, a lost-response retry, a legitimate re-checkout correction, two concurrent corrections, and two corrections sharing one frozen millisecond, bracketed by two negative controls. Demonstrating it in production would require firing concurrent checkouts at a real booking, which the owner explicitly forbade and which would risk a real client's balance. **Do not record this half as production-proven on the strength of the tip confirmation.**

## R-2026-09-19-A — `CANCEL-EMAIL-PAYMENT-COPY`: the cancellation email no longer says "No charge has been made" to a paying customer · 1-unit release (`functions:salown`, 2 functions) · **ARTIFACT_VERIFIED · + BL-4 `autoRefundEnabled` armed and left ON**

- **Why:** every cancellation email said "No charge has been made"; on 2026-09-18 the customer auto-refunded by BL-4 (£10 deposit, `o0ogoI1ztu7H7QFiGziW`) read it three seconds before Stripe accepted the refund.
- **Rule shipped:** the money sentence is decided from the booking READ AT SEND TIME. Only a STARTED refund (`IN_PROGRESS`, `SUCCEEDED`, inline Connect refund, reconciled full Dashboard refund) → "Your £X payment is being refunded to your original payment method. Refunds usually take 5–10 working days to appear." — never "has been refunded" (the actuator writes `SUCCEEDED` for a still-`pending` Stripe refund). Bare `REQUESTED` intent, `FAILED`, `NEEDS_REVIEW`, partial refund, unreadable booking, money-without-refund → no money sentence. No money at all → historic "No charge" (byte-identical). Flag and cancellation are not inputs. EN + TR.
- **Owner approval (2026-09-19):** targeted deploy of these two functions from `79e232f` only; no test booking, no cancel; then arm the flag and leave it on after a backlog check.
- **Candidate:** `rc/cancel-email-copy-on-c8a64d6` @ **`79e232f`** on base **`c8a64d6`**. Drift check immediately before release: both live GCS source zips (gen `1789223780893054`, `1789223780899331`) — non-test `src/` + `package.json` byte-identical to `c8a64d6`.
- **Invariance:** `salownCancelByToken` cancel / window / Connect refund / intent write / audit part hashes identical to live (`9106fcea…`); the only change inside it is one extra booking read inside the existing email try-block. `functions/src/payments/` untouched.
- **Gates (pinned workspace = fresh clones side by side: `salown-app` detached at `79e232f` + `whitecross-site`, own `npm ci`):** build + tsc 0 · functions 2699 pass / 0 fail / 42 skipped · new suite 16/16 · archive manifest ok (228 files, 0 untracked, 0 forbidden, no `.secret*`/`.env*` in workspace) · wrapper `--check-only` OK.
- **Release:** `./scripts/deploy-functions.sh salownCancelByToken salownSendCancellationEmail` → **`salowncancelbytoken-00070-moq` → `-00071-xaw`** (upd `2026-09-19T09:11:59.91Z`) · **`salownsendcancellationemail-00104-hib` → `-00105-hiz`** (upd `09:11:56.58Z`).
- **Post-deploy:** inventory 124 → 124, exactly these two `updateTime`s moved; us-central1 30. Config drift 0 on both (entry, runtime, 256Mi/1cpu, timeout, max 20, concurrency 80, ingress, SA, secrets+versions, env keys). Deployed zips: `src/` AND `lib/` byte-identical to the workspace; `cancellationPaymentNote` ×3 in `lib/index.js`; `lib/emails/cancellationCopy.js` present; 0 secret-like entries. Deployed module executed offline on 8 booking shapes: unpaid → No charge · REQUESTED → silent · IN_PROGRESS / SUCCEEDED → "is being refunded" £10.00 · FAILED / NEEDS_REVIEW / paid-panel-cancel / unreadable → silent. Armed with `activateAutoRefund.mjs --arm --expect-update-time 2026-09-18T14:15:33.847855Z` → `2026-09-19T09:13:36.635583Z`, one field `false → true`, audit `refundflag_1789809216565`. First armed sweeper pass `09:15:14Z`: `scanned 0, spent 0, skipped 0`. **Left ON by owner decision.**

- **main:** aligned by merge `6ea289c` [skip ci] of `integrate/cancel-email-copy-main` @ `1097b02` (revert `e051697` + apply `79e232f`); on the merged tree with the whitecross-site sibling present: functions 2774 pass / 0 fail, new suite 16/16; module + both handlers + cancellation lines byte-identical to the DEPLOYED source. ## R-2026-09-16-A — `PLATFORM-DEPOSIT-SERVICE-EDIT`: a Booksy/Fresha platform-deposit booking may change service; the deposit is kept · 1-unit release (`functions:salown`, 1 function) · **ARTIFACT_VERIFIED · behaviour proven on the emulator through the real callable · production service swap done by the owner in the panel (owner-reported "düzeldi", not re-read by this session)**

- **Why:** the owner could not change a paid-deposit (£10) Booksy booking from "Skin Fade Cut" (£32) to "Full Skin Fade & Beard Luxury" (£48) before checkout — refusal `EDIT_WOULD_DISCARD_COLLECTED_MONEY` / `FOREIGN_PREPAYMENT`. A product regression for the owner since the C1 Admin release `cde349264ff973c8` (2026-09-08): a need that worked before was blocked by C1's deliberate foreign-rail limit (`1d74945`).
- **Owner approval:** targeted deploy of `salownPatchBookingDetails` only, conditional on the checks below; no other function, no hosting.
- **Source:** `release/platform-deposit-on-live-65ad272` @ **`42f78a7`** = live source `65ad272` + cherry-pick of `57c0cdd` only (`platformDepositEdit.ts` new, `priceEditExecutor.ts` +14/−1, test). The `advanceTx.ts` / `utils/presentation.ts` changes on main were deliberately NOT shipped.
- **Pre-deploy proof:** the previous live package (source generation `1788866366777917`) was byte-identical to the `65ad272` functions tree (113/113 files). Candidate: `tsc` 0; functions 2655 tests / 0 fail / 42 skipped; targeted 174/174; archive manifest clean; `lib/` diff vs previous live = exactly the 2 intended files. Emulator via `salownPatchBookingDetails.run`, 5/5: service, price 48, duration 45 and endTime written; `paidAmount`, `platformDepositAmount`, `paymentType` unchanged; the live checkout reader `resolveDeskPrePaid` gives due 38; a below-deposit total → `REFUND_OR_CREDIT_REQUIRED` with zero writes; the Stripe rail is still refused; the form callable is still refused; a replay writes nothing. The same test against the previous live `lib/` bytes: 3/5 fail with the owner's refusal.
- **Real record (read-only, required fields only):** the shape matches the lane (Booksy, CONFIRMED, DEPOSIT, deposit 10 = paidAmount 10, no provider/marker/remaining/tip/split/checkout, no discount/discountSnapshot). Target service £48 / 45 min, active, no variations. No overlap for the barber in the extended 18:20–18:30 window. The record was not modified by the deploying session.
- **Deploy:** `./scripts/deploy-functions.sh salownPatchBookingDetails` from an isolated clone, 2026-09-16 19:56 UK (updateTime 18:56:57Z). Revision **`salownpatchbookingdetails-00002-zek`**, source generation `1789584942769296`, `firebase-functions-hash` `e1e24baf091d1f88c432d3f1ff3ab875d5a21988`, ACTIVE; memory/timeout/service account unchanged.
- **Post-deploy:** inventory 124 → 124; only this function changed revision (europe-west2 94, us-central1 30, otherwise identical). Deployed `lib/` byte-identical to the candidate; `package.json` and lock unchanged; no secret/env/log file in the package.
- **Not changed:** `salownEditBookingForm` stays on its previous revision (the patch does not change its behaviour); hosting and rules untouched.
- **Rollback:** redeploy source `65ad272` from an isolated clone with `./scripts/deploy-functions.sh salownPatchBookingDetails`. Previous revision `salownpatchbookingdetails-00001-yur`, source generation `1788866366777917`.
- **Open:** a service swap does not re-evaluate a Booksy happy-hour `discount`/`discountSnapshot` (not present on this record). `57c0cdd` (`fix/platform-deposit-service-edit`) is not on main yet — a later functions deploy of this executor from main must include it or it regresses this release.

## R-2026-09-15-A — `STAFF-AVAIL-GAP-P2` + `WALKIN-BACKDATE-FLOOR`: Staff App Walk-in server-side D1-D7 callable transition + checkout-instant backdate fix · 2-unit release (`functions:salown` 1 function + `hosting:salown-staff`) · **LIVE_VERIFIED (source + served-byte + reachability; behaviour proven earlier against a local emulator and real Chrome clicks against an isolated rehearsal, not re-proven against real production data)**

Staff App Walk-in (Save and Save & Checkout) now creates through the server-authoritative `salownCreateStaffWalkIn` callable instead of a direct client write, enforcing the same D1-D7 contract as Phase 1 New Booking (shared `functions/src/bookings/staffPolicyGate.ts`): passive/undated-leave a HARD stop for every role; conflict and hours reject-by-default with an `owner`-only, reason-required, atomically-audited override; a `BLOCKED` (mola) collision never overridable. Plus `WALKIN-BACKDATE-FLOOR`: the untouched-time Save & Checkout backdate no longer floors at 09:00 — it computes one absolute instant (checkout-now minus duration) and correctly rolls the calendar day back across midnight, with a companion `assertAssignableStaff` `historicalExemptionAllowed:false` fix so a midnight-crossing backdate on this surface still gates `STAFF_PASSIVE`/`STAFF_NOT_STARTED` |

## R-2026-08-30-G — `CLIENT-SPEND-PREDESK` · 1-unit release (`hosting:salown`)

**Owner-approved, one hosting site, browser-only change.** The client's spend history counts
the money taken before the desk. **No function, no rules, no indexes, no other site, no
Firestore write.**

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Owner authorisation** | *"Client spend düzeltmesini içeren temiz, SHA-sabitli kaynaktan yalnız hosting:salown deploy et. Functions, rules, indexes, salown-staff veya diğer hosting target'larına dokunma. Deploy sonrası Jonathan'ın client history satırının £32 ve lifetime spend değerinin doğru olduğunu sunulan bundle üzerinden doğrula. BookingDetailPanel.tsx kilitli olduğu için ödeme etiketi ve stale service alanına dokunma."* |
| **Work ID / source** | `CLIENT-SPEND-PREDESK` · salown-app **`93c5c8a`** (clean, SHA-pinned `git archive` + `npm ci` workspace) |
| **Why** | The owner opened a repaired booking's client and found a £32 visit recorded as **£4.00**. The Clients page computed spend inline, twice, from `paidAmount` alone — the money settled before the desk lives in `platformDepositAmount`, and **`Clients.tsx` was not on BL-8's own list of readers that add it back** |
| **Why it was invisible** | Two errors cancelled: a full-online-paid booking used to be billed AGAIN at the desk, so `paidAmount` accidentally held the whole sale. R-2026-08-30-D/E made the till truthful, which removed the second error hiding the first. The history ROW was always wrong regardless — it never had the aggregate's DEPOSIT branch, so every website deposit booking showed the desk's £22 of a £32 visit |
| **Blast radius, measured** | Over all **1669** non-cancelled whitecross bookings, old rule vs new: lifetime-spend aggregate changes **1 row (+£28.00)**; booking-history row changes **20 rows (+£240.00)**. All under-reports becoming correct; **nothing decreases** |
| **What changed** | Both call sites go through the new `src/pages/clientSpend.ts`. Aggregator rows deliberately untouched — a Booksy deposit resolves from tenant CONFIG, not the document (`preDeskAmount` returns 0 for Booksy), because a parsed row often carries no deposit field and letting both speak is how one starts double-counting |
